package life.ctrl.render;

/** Visible cutaway boundaries in gameplay units. Shared by renderer and visual QA. */
public final class SceneLayout {
  private SceneLayout() {}

  public record Bounds(int x, int y, int width, int height) {}

  public static Bounds bounds(String map) {
    return switch (map) {
      case "room" -> new Bounds(137, 57, 206, 206);
      case "stair" -> new Bounds(90, 25, 301, 254);
      case "cafe", "diner", "workshop" -> new Bounds(89, 41, 302, 230);
      case "park" -> new Bounds(24, 56, 432, 213);
      case "street" -> new Bounds(16, 48, 928, 417);
      default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown scene: " + map);
    };
  }
}

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/render/AudioScene.java

package life.ctrl.render;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.audio.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Disposable;
import life.ctrl.model.Settings;

public final class AudioScene implements Disposable {
  private Music music, rain;
  private Sound click, step, door, success;

  public AudioScene() {
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio")) return;
    try {
      music = Gdx.audio.newMusic(Gdx.files.internal("audio/evening.wav"));
      rain = Gdx.audio.newMusic(Gdx.files.internal("audio/rain.wav"));
      click = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/click.wav"));
      step = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/step.wav"));
      door = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/door.wav"));
      success = Gdx.audio.newSound(Gdx.files.internal("audio/success.wav"));
      music.setLooping(true);
      rain.setLooping(true);
      music.play();
      rain.play();
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
      Gdx.app.log("Audio", "Аудиоустройство недоступно; игра продолжит работу.");
      dispose();
    }
  }

  public boolean ready() {
    return music != null
        && rain != null
        && click != null
        && step != null
        && door != null
        && success != null;
  }

  public void update(Settings s, boolean outside, boolean focused) {
    if (music != null) music.setVolume(focused ? s.music * .65f : 0);
    if (rain != null) rain.setVolume(focused ? s.sfx * (outside ? .9f : .18f) : 0);
  }

  public void door(Settings s) {
    if (door != null) door.play(s.sfx * .7f);
  }

  public void success(Settings s) {
    if (success != null) success.play(s.sfx * .6f);
  }

  public void click(Settings s) {
    if (click != null) click.play(s.sfx * .5f);
  }

  public void step(Settings s) {
    if (step != null) step.play(s.sfx * .3f);
  }

  public void dispose() {
    if (door != null) {
      door.dispose();
      door = null;
    }
    if (success != null) {
      success.dispose();
      success = null;
    }
    if (music != null) {
      music.dispose();
      music = null;
    }
    if (rain != null) {
      rain.dispose();
      rain = null;
    }
    if (click != null) {
      click.dispose();
      click = null;
    }
    if (step != null) {
      step.dispose();
      step = null;
    }
  }
}

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3Application;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3WindowAdapter;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;

public final class DesktopLauncher {
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setTitle("CTRL+LIFE — Тихий район");
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.setWindowIcon("art/icon32.png", "art/icon64.png", "art/icon128.png");
    config.setWindowSizeLimits(960, 540, -1, -1);
    config.useVsync(true);
    config.setForegroundFPS(60);
    config.setIdleFPS(15);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio")) config.disableAudio(true);
    var game = new CtrlLife();
    config.setWindowListener(
        new Lwjgl3WindowAdapter() {
          @Override
          public void focusLost() {
            game.focusChanged(false);
          }

          @Override
          public void focusGained() {
            game.focusChanged(true);
          }
        });
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, config);
  }
}

package life.ctrl.content;

import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;

/** Content contains no rendering objects and can be validated in headless tests. */
public final class Content {
  public MapDef[] maps;
  public Dialogue[] dialogues;

  public static final class MapDef {
    public String id, name, subtitle;
    public int width, height;
    public float spawnX, spawnY;
    public float[][] solids;
    public Prop[] objects;
    public Npc[] npcs;
    public Sign[] signs;
  }

  public static final class Sign {
    public String text, color;
    public float x, y;
  }

  public static final class Prop {
    public String id, label, action, art, target;
    public float x, y, artX, artY, targetX, targetY;
    public float[] solid;
  }

  public static final class Npc {
    public String id, name, art, dialogue;
    public float[][] route;
  }

  public static final class Dialogue {
    public String id, speaker, text;
    public Choice[] choices;
  }

  public static final class Choice {
    public String label, next, effect;
  }

  public static Content load() {
    try (var in = Content.class.getResourceAsStream("/data/world.json")) {
      if (in == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Не найден world.json");
      Content c =
          new Json().fromJson(Content.class, new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      c.validate();
      return c;
    } catch (IOException e) {
      throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
    }
  }

  public MapDef map(String id) {
    for (var m : maps) if (m.id.equals(id)) return m;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Карта: " + id);
  }

  public Dialogue dialogue(String id) {
    for (var d : dialogues) if (d.id.equals(id)) return d;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Диалог: " + id);
  }

  public void validate() {
    Set<String> mapIds = new HashSet<>(), dialogueIds = new HashSet<>(), npcIds = new HashSet<>();
    for (var d : dialogues)
      if (!dialogueIds.add(d.id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Повтор диалога: " + d.id);
    for (var m : maps)
      if (!mapIds.add(m.id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Повтор карты: " + m.id);
    for (var m : maps) {
      Set<String> props = new HashSet<>();
      for (var p : m.objects) {
        if (!props.add(p.id)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Повтор объекта: " + p.id);
        if (p.target != null && !mapIds.contains(p.target))
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Сломанный переход: " + p.id);
      }
      for (var n : m.npcs) {
        if (!npcIds.add(n.id) || !dialogueIds.contains(n.dialogue) || n.route.length < 2)
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("NPC: " + n.id);
      }
    }
    for (var d : dialogues)
      for (var c : d.choices)
        if (!c.next.isEmpty() && !dialogueIds.contains(c.next))
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Ссылка диалога: " + c.next);
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/content/Content.java

FILE: core/src/main/java/life/ctrl/content/LifeContent.java

package life.ctrl.content;

import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;

public final class LifeContent {
  public Girl[] girls;
  public Item[] items;
  public Job[] jobs;

  public static final class Girl {
    public String id,
        name,
        about,
        values,
        likes,
        portrait,
        homeMap,
        introduction,
        commitLine,
        notReady,
        nightLine,
        story;
    public int age;
    public String[] favoriteGifts, venues;
    public Topic[] topics;
    public DateChoice[] dateChoices;
  }

  public static final class Topic {
    public String id, label, response;
    public int affinity, trust;
  }

  public static final class DateChoice {
    public String label, response;
    public int affinity, trust;
  }

  public static final class Item {
    public String id, name, kind, description;
    public int price, hunger, energy;
  }

  public static final class Job {
    public String id,
        title,
        brief,
        function,
        source,
        kind = "feature",
        client = "Клиент",
        feedback = "",
        hint = "";
    public String[] solutions, initialSources, patches;
    public double[] seed;
    public int tier, xp = 1, cost, operationBudget;
    public boolean once;
    public String[] algorithms;
    public int pay, minutes;
    public Case[] tests;
  }

  public static final class Case {
    public double[] args;
    public double expected;
  }

  public static LifeContent load() {
    try (var in = LifeContent.class.getResourceAsStream("/data/life.json")) {
      if (in == null) throw new IOException("Нет life.json");
      var c =
          new Json()
              .fromJson(LifeContent.class, new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      Set<String> ids = new HashSet<>();
      for (var g : c.girls)
        if (g.age < 21 || !ids.add(g.id) || g.topics.length < 3 || g.dateChoices.length < 2)
          throw new IOException("Некорректная героиня");
      ids.clear();
      for (var j : c.jobs) {
        if (!ids.add(j.id)
            || j.pay < 0
            || j.cost < 0
            || j.minutes <= 0
            || j.minutes > 720
            || j.solutions == null
            || j.solutions.length != 3
            || j.initialSources.length != 3
            || j.patches.length != 3
            || j.tests.length == 0
            || j.seed.length == 0) throw new IOException("Некорректный заказ: " + j.id);
      }
      return c;
    } catch (IOException e) {
      throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
    }
  }

  public Girl girl(String id) {
    for (var g : girls) if (g.id.equals(id)) return g;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Героиня: " + id);
  }

  public Item item(String id) {
    for (var i : items) if (i.id.equals(id)) return i;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Предмет: " + id);
  }

  public Job job(String id) {
    for (var j : jobs) if (j.id.equals(id)) return j;
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Заказ: " + id);
  }
}

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/AssetContractTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.regex.*;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** Asset and text contracts catch failures that Java compilation cannot see. */
class AssetContractTest {
  private java.awt.image.BufferedImage image(String id) throws IOException {
    try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/art/" + id + ".png")) {
      assertNotNull(in, "Missing PNG: " + id);
      var png = ImageIO.read(in);
      assertNotNull(png, "Invalid PNG: " + id);
      return png;
    }
  }

  @Test
  void everyMapAndObjectHasARealReadableTexture() throws Exception {
    var content = Content.load();
    for (var m : content.maps) {
      var png = image(m.id);
      assertEquals(m.width, png.getWidth());
      assertEquals(m.height, png.getHeight());
      for (var p : m.objects) if (p.art != null) image(p.art);
      for (var n : m.npcs) image(n.art);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void animationAndStateVariantsMatchRuntimeGeometry() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("hero", "hero_1", "hero_2", "mira", "arkady", "lev")) {
      var png = image(id);
      assertEquals(64, png.getWidth(), id);
      assertEquals(96, png.getHeight(), id);
      assertTrue(png.getColorModel().hasAlpha(), id);
    }
    for (String[] pair :
        new String[][] {
          {"boxes", "boxes_open"}, {"plant", "plant_watered"}, {"mailbox", "mailbox_signed"}
        }) {
      var base = image(pair[0]);
      var variant = image(pair[1]);
      assertEquals(base.getWidth(), variant.getWidth());
      assertEquals(base.getHeight(), variant.getHeight());
    }
    for (String id :
        List.of(
            "portrait_hero", "portrait_mira", "portrait_arkady", "portrait_lev", "portrait_note")) {
      var png = image(id);
      assertEquals(32, png.getWidth());
      assertEquals(32, png.getHeight());
    }
  }

  private Map<Integer, Integer> advances() throws IOException {
    try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/fonts/ui.fnt")) {
      assertNotNull(in);
      String fnt = new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      assertTrue(fnt.contains("padding=0,0,0,0"));
      Map<Integer, Integer> a = new HashMap<>();
      var matcher = Pattern.compile("char id=(\\d+).*?xadvance=(\\d+)").matcher(fnt);
      while (matcher.find())
        a.put(Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(1)), Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(2)));
      return a;
    }
  }

  @Test
  void cyrillicDialogueFitsBesidePortraitWithoutMissingGlyphs() throws Exception {
    var widths = advances();
    for (char c : "ЁёЙй×·№→←↑↓".toCharArray()) assertTrue(widths.containsKey((int) c));
    for (var node : Content.load().dialogues) {
      int lines = 1, used = 0;
      for (String word : node.text.split(" ")) {
        int width = widths.get(32);
        for (char c : word.toCharArray()) {
          assertTrue(widths.containsKey((int) c), "Missing glyph: " + c);
          width += widths.get((int) c);
        }
        if (used + width > 378) {
          lines++;
          used = width;
        } else used += width;
      }
      assertTrue(lines <= 3, node.id + " overflows into response buttons: " + lines + " lines");
    }
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/AssetContractTest.java

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/CareerTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.bytescript.ByteScript;
import life.ctrl.content.*;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class CareerTest {
  final LifeContent data = LifeContent.load();
  @TempDir Path folder;

  void finish(GameState s, String id) {
    var j = data.job(id);
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.repair(s, j, Arrays.asList(j.solutions).indexOf(j.source));
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed(), id);
    Coding.preview(s, j, j.seed[j.seed.length - 1]);
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(5, s.jobStage, id);
  }

  @Test
  void tactileDraftAndDebugRequireSeparateTypingPhases() {
    var state = new GameState();
    var job = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(state, job, 0, false, true);
    assertEquals(0, state.typedCount);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(state, job).compiled());
    while (state.typedCount < state.codeSource.length()) Coding.type(state);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(state, job).passed());

    Coding.repair(state, job, 0, true);
    assertEquals(0, state.typedCount);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(state, job).compiled());
    while (state.typedCount < state.codeSource.length()) Coding.type(state);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(state, job).passed());
  }

  @Test
  void profileCountsExecutedOperationsAndRejectsSlowCorrectProgram() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.codingXp = 8;
    var j = data.job("batch");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    var r = Coding.test(s, j);
    assertTrue(r.compiled());
    assertFalse(r.passed());
    assertTrue(r.cases().stream().allMatch(x -> x.endsWith("OK")));
    assertEquals(7, ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource).operations(3));
    Coding.repair(s, j, 1);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed());
    assertEquals(1, ByteScript.compile(s.codeSource).operations(3));
  }

  @Test
  void profilerOnlyVisitsTakenBranch() {
    var p = ByteScript.compile("fn f(x) { return x > 0 ? x + 1 : x * x + x; }");
    assertEquals(3, p.operations(2));
    assertEquals(4, p.operations(-2));
  }

  @Test
  void revisionsRestoreExactSourceAndInvalidateRelease() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.commit(s);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    var store = new SaveStore(folder, Content.load());
    store.save(s);
    s = store.load().state();
    assertEquals(2, s.revisions.size());
    Coding.restore(s, 0);
    assertFalse(s.programPreviewed);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(s, j).compiled());
    Coding.restore(s, 1);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed());
    assertFalse(s.programPreviewed);
  }

  @Test
  void repeatedCommitsAndStockTestsCannotFarmQualityBonus() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.commit(s);
    Coding.commit(s);
    assertEquals(2, s.revisions.size());
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {70, 2});
    assertTrue(s.customCases.isEmpty());
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 8});
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 8});
    assertEquals(1, s.customCases.size());
    assertEquals(0, Career.bonus(s, j));
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 0});
    assertEquals(100, Career.bonus(s, j));
  }

  @Test
  void probeErrorDoesNotGrantQualityCredit() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {12, 8});
    assertTrue(s.customCases.isEmpty());
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.probe(s, j, new double[] {Double.NaN, 8});
    assertTrue(s.customCases.isEmpty());
  }

  @Test
  void rushDeadlinePaysBonusOnlyWhenWorkFits() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0, true);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    assertEquals(j.pay / 4, Career.bonus(s, j));
    Life.advance(s, 91);
    assertEquals(0, Career.bonus(s, j));
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(10650, s.money);
    assertTrue(s.lastClientMessage.contains("Тамара"));
  }

  @Test
  void unfinishedProjectCannotBeSilentlyReplaced() {
    var s = new GameState();
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("total"), 0);
    String before = s.codeSource;
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("discount"), 0);
    assertEquals("total", s.activeJob);
    assertEquals(before, s.codeSource);
    Coding.abandon(s);
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("discount"), 0);
    assertEquals("discount", s.activeJob);
    assertEquals(10000, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void progressionOpensNewWorkButCapsDailyIncome() {
    var s = new GameState();
    assertEquals(3, Career.offers(s, data).size());
    finish(s, "total");
    finish(s, "discount");
    finish(s, "delivery");
    assertEquals(1, Career.tier(s));
    assertEquals(6, Career.offers(s, data).size());
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("refund"), 0);
    assertNotEquals("refund", s.activeJob);
    Life.sleep(s);
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("refund"), 0);
    assertEquals("refund", s.activeJob);
    assertEquals(2, s.jobStage);
  }

  @Test
  void offerRotationIsStableAndChangesNextDay() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.codingXp = 8;
    var first = Career.offers(s, data).stream().map(j -> j.id).toList();
    assertEquals(first, Career.offers(s, data).stream().map(j -> j.id).toList());
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertNotEquals(first, Career.offers(s, data).stream().map(j -> j.id).toList());
  }

  @Test
  void upgradesHaveRealEffectAndAreOneTimePurchases() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    int before = Career.duration(s, j);
    Career.buy(s, "ssd");
    assertEquals(5800, s.money);
    assertTrue(Career.duration(s, j) < before);
    Career.buy(s, "ssd");
    assertEquals(5800, s.money);
    Career.buy(s, "chair");
    assertEquals(3000, s.money);
    var poor = new GameState();
    poor.money = 0;
    Career.buy(poor, "ssd");
    assertTrue(poor.upgrades.isEmpty());
  }

  @Test
  void projectRequiresOrderedShippedCodeAndPaysOnlyAfterLaunch() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.completedOrders = 1;
    Coding.accept(s, data.job("support"), 0);
    assertTrue(s.activeJob.isEmpty());
    finish(s, "board");
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(9700, s.money);
    finish(s, "remind");
    finish(s, "support");
    assertEquals(9100, s.money);
    int day = s.day();
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(day + 1, s.day());
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
    Career.settleProject(s);
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
    var store = new SaveStore(folder, Content.load());
    store.save(s);
    s = store.load().state();
    Career.settleProject(s);
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
    Coding.deliver(s, data.job("support"));
    assertEquals(9260, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void unaffordableLaunchKeepsWorkingCodeUntilPlayerCanPay() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.completedOrders = 1;
    s.money = 0;
    var j = data.job("board");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(0, Career.projectStage(s));
    assertEquals(3, s.jobStage);
    Economy.earn(s, 300, "Подработка");
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(1, Career.projectStage(s));
    assertEquals(0, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void versionTwoMigrationPreservesLifeAndRestoresCareer() throws Exception {
    Files.writeString(
        folder.resolve("save.json"),
        "{\"version\":2,\"state\":{\"name\":\"Саша\",\"money\":4321,\"paidJobs\":[\"1:total\",\"2:discount\"]}}");
    var s = new SaveStore(folder, Content.load()).load().state();
    assertEquals(4321, s.money);
    assertEquals(2, s.codingXp);
    assertEquals(2, s.completedOrders);
    assertTrue(s.shipped.contains("discount"));
  }

  @Test
  void duskAndWeatherStayDeterministicAndReadable() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 12 * 60;
    assertEquals(0, Atmosphere.night(s));
    s.gameMinutes = 19 * 60;
    assertTrue(Atmosphere.night(s) > .3 && Atmosphere.night(s) < .5);
    s.gameMinutes = 23 * 60;
    assertEquals(1, Atmosphere.night(s));
    assertTrue(Atmosphere.rain(s));
    s.gameMinutes = 1440 + 12 * 60;
    assertFalse(Atmosphere.rain(s));
  }

  @Test
  void overnightLaunchOnlyPaysOnFollowingDay() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.completedOrders = 1;
    s.shipped.addAll(List.of("board", "remind"));
    s.gameMinutes = 23 * 60;
    finish(s, "support");
    assertEquals(2, s.day());
    assertEquals(9700, s.money);
    assertEquals(2, s.projectIncomeDay);
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(9700, s.money);
    Life.sleep(s);
    assertEquals(9860, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void cancellingDoesNotResetPromisedRushDeadline() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0, true);
    int promised = s.rushDeadline;
    Life.advance(s, 100);
    Coding.abandon(s);
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0, true);
    assertEquals(promised, s.rushDeadline);
    assertEquals(0, Career.bonus(s, j));
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/CareerTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import life.ctrl.bytescript.ByteScript;
import life.ctrl.content.*;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class LifeSystemsTest {
  final LifeContent data = LifeContent.load();
  final Content content = Content.load();
  @TempDir Path folder;

  @Test
  void startsWithTenThousandAndAllPricesAreAffordableButMeaningful() {
    var s = new GameState();
    assertEquals(10000, s.money);
    assertEquals(3000, Economy.WEEKLY_RENT);
    for (var j : data.jobs) if (j.tier == 0 && !j.once) assertTrue(j.pay >= 650 && j.pay <= 950);
    assertTrue(data.item("soup").price < 650);
  }

  @Test
  void purchaseAndFoodAreSingleInventoryTransactions() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var meal = data.item("soup");
    Life.buy(s, meal);
    assertEquals(10000 - meal.price, s.money);
    assertEquals(1, s.count("soup"));
    double h = s.hunger;
    Life.eat(s, meal);
    assertEquals(0, s.count("soup"));
    assertTrue(s.hunger > h);
    int money = s.money;
    Life.eat(s, meal);
    assertEquals(money, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void purchaseRejectsOverspending() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.money = 1;
    Life.buy(s, data.item("book"));
    assertEquals(1, s.money);
    assertEquals(0, s.count("book"));
  }

  @Test
  void weeklyRentOccursOnceAcrossSaveAndLoad() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 3 * 1440 - 1;
    Life.advance(s, 2);
    assertEquals(7000, s.money);
    assertEquals(10, s.rentThroughDay);
    var saves = new SaveStore(folder, content);
    saves.save(s);
    s = saves.load().state();
    Life.advance(s, 1);
    assertEquals(7000, s.money);
    assertEquals(1, s.transactions.size());
  }

  @Test
  void povertyHasFoodAndIncomeWithoutAPc() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.money = 0;
    s.hunger = 1;
    Life.freeMeal(s);
    assertTrue(s.hunger >= 40);
    Life.courier(s);
    assertEquals(240, s.money);
    assertTrue(s.items.isEmpty());
    Life.courier(s);
    assertEquals(240, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void everyTemplateReallyCompilesAndPassesItsClientCases() {
    for (var j : data.jobs) {
      var p = ByteScript.compile(j.source);
      assertEquals(j.function, p.name());
      for (var c : j.tests) assertEquals(c.expected, p.run(c.args), .001);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void syntaxAndSemanticAndTestFailuresAreDifferent() {
    assertEquals(
        ByteScript.Kind.SYNTAX,
        assertThrows(ByteScript.Error.class, () -> ByteScript.compile("fn f(x) { return ; }"))
            .kind);
    assertEquals(
        ByteScript.Kind.SEMANTIC,
        assertThrows(ByteScript.Error.class, () -> ByteScript.compile("fn f(x) { return y; }"))
            .kind);
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 1);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    var r = Coding.test(s, j);
    assertTrue(r.compiled());
    assertFalse(r.passed());
  }

  @Test
  void realRepairChangesProgramAndPaymentCannotBeDuplicatedEvenAcrossMidnight() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 23 * 60 + 50;
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(s, j).compiled());
    String before = s.codeSource;
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    assertNotEquals(before, s.codeSource);
    assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed());
    assertEquals(90, Coding.preview(s, j, 3));
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    int paid = s.money;
    assertEquals(10650, paid);
    assertEquals(2, s.day());
    Coding.deliver(s, j);
    assertEquals(paid, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void projectNeedsPreviewAndSurvivesSavingInTheMiddle() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.type(s);
    var saves = new SaveStore(folder, content);
    saves.save(s);
    var copy = saves.load().state();
    assertEquals(s.typedCount, copy.typedCount);
    assertEquals(s.codeSource, copy.codeSource);
    Coding.deliver(copy, j);
    assertEquals(10000, copy.money);
  }

  @Test
  void favouriteGiftsDoNotBuyTrustAndCannotBeSpammed() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    s.items.put("seeds", 2);
    Social.gift(s, g, data.item("seeds"));
    var r = s.relation("mira");
    int a = r.affinity;
    Social.gift(s, g, data.item("seeds"));
    assertEquals(a, r.affinity);
    assertEquals(1, s.count("seeds"));
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    assertTrue(s.plannedGirl.isEmpty());
  }

  @Test
  void topicsAreUniqueAndRepeatedConversationsDoNotFarmAffection() {
    var s = new GameState();
    for (var g : data.girls) {
      Social.meet(s, g);
      Social.talk(s, g, 0);
      var r = s.relation(g.id);
      int a = r.affinity;
      Social.talk(s, g, 0);
      assertEquals(a, r.affinity);
      assertTrue(r.trust >= 8);
    }
    assertNotEquals(data.girl("mira").topics[0].response, data.girl("asya").topics[0].response);
  }

  @Test
  void datesRequireInvitationsAndMoneyAndFreeParkIsPossible() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("asya");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(0, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    Social.invite(s, g, "cafe");
    s.map = "cafe";
    s.money = 0;
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(0, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    s.plannedVenue = "park";
    s.map = "park";
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(1, s.relation(g.id).dates);
    assertEquals(0, s.money);
  }

  @Test
  void everyGirlHasAReachableRelationshipAndConsentPath() {
    for (var g : data.girls) {
      var s = new GameState();
      for (int day = 1; day <= 4; day++) {
        s.gameMinutes = (day - 1) * 1440 + 18 * 60;
        Social.meet(s, g);
        Social.talk(s, g, 0);
        Social.invite(s, g, "park");
        s.map = "park";
        Social.date(s, g, 0);
        if (day == 3) Social.commit(s, g);
      }
      assertEquals(g.id, s.partnerId);
      assertTrue(Social.canShareNight(s, g));
      s.map = "room";
      int nights = s.relation(g.id).sharedNights;
      Social.shareNight(s, g, false);
      assertEquals(nights, s.relation(g.id).sharedNights);
      Social.shareNight(s, g, true);
      assertEquals(nights + 1, s.relation(g.id).sharedNights);
      assertTrue(s.energy > 90);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void friendshipNeverImplicitlyConsentsToIntimacy() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    var r = s.relation(g.id);
    r.affinity = 100;
    r.trust = 100;
    r.dates = 10;
    assertFalse(Social.canShareNight(s, g));
    Social.shareNight(s, g, true);
    assertEquals(0, r.sharedNights);
  }

  @Test
  void socialStateAndLedgerRoundTrip() throws Exception {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("nika");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Life.buy(s, data.item("book"));
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    var store = new SaveStore(folder, content);
    store.save(s);
    var copy = store.load().state();
    assertEquals(s.relation(g.id).trust, copy.relation(g.id).trust);
    assertEquals(g.id, copy.plannedGirl);
    assertEquals(s.transactions.get(0).amount, copy.transactions.get(0).amount);
    assertEquals(1, copy.count("book"));
  }

  @Test
  void oneNpcExistsInOnePlaceDuringTheDate() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    var w = new World(content, s);
    int count = 0;
    for (var m : content.maps) {
      s.map = m.id;
      count += w.npcs().stream().filter(n -> n.id.equals("mira")).count();
    }
    assertEquals(1, count);
  }

  @Test
  void legacyRoomSaveMigratesToSafeSmallerRoom() throws Exception {
    String old =
        "{\"version\":1,\"state\":{\"name\":\"Саша\",\"map\":\"room\",\"x\":380,\"y\":210}}";
    Files.writeString(folder.resolve("save.json"), old);
    var state = new SaveStore(folder, content).load().state();
    assertEquals(240, state.x);
    assertEquals(190, state.y);
    assertEquals(10000, state.money);
  }

  @Test
  void allAlgorithmChoicesCanBeRepairedAndPaid() {
    for (var j : data.jobs)
      for (int choice = 0; choice < j.algorithms.length; choice++) {
        var s = new GameState();
        s.codingXp = 8;
        s.completedOrders = 1;
        if (j.id.equals("remind") || j.id.equals("support")) s.shipped.add("board");
        if (j.id.equals("support")) s.shipped.add("remind");
        Coding.accept(s, j, choice);
        Coding.completeTyping(s);
        Coding.repair(s, j, java.util.Arrays.asList(j.solutions).indexOf(j.source));
        assertTrue(Coding.test(s, j).passed(), j.id + ":" + choice);
        Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
        Coding.deliver(s, j);
        assertEquals(10000 + j.pay - j.cost, s.money);
      }
  }

  @Test
  void cannotCollectADifferentJobsFee() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var j = data.job("total");
    Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
    Coding.completeTyping(s);
    Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
    Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
    Coding.deliver(s, data.job("discount"));
    assertEquals(10000, s.money);
    assertFalse(Coding.test(s, data.job("discount")).passed());
  }

  @Test
  void datesCannotHappenRemotely() {
    var s = new GameState();
    var g = data.girl("mira");
    Social.meet(s, g);
    Social.talk(s, g, 0);
    Social.invite(s, g, "park");
    Social.date(s, g, 0);

    assertEquals("mira", s.plannedGirl);
    s.map = "park";
    Social.date(s, g, 0);
    assertEquals(1, s.relation(g.id).dates);
  }

  @Test
  void rejectsNonFiniteNeedsAndBrokenInventory() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.health = Double.NaN;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
    s.health = 85;
    s.items.put("soup", -1);
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
  }

  @Test
  void twoDailyOrdersCoverTwoMealsAndRentForTwoWeeks() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.gameMinutes = 480;
    for (int day = 1; day <= 14; day++) {
      for (String id : new String[] {"total", "discount"}) {
        var j = data.job(id);
        Coding.accept(s, j, 0);
        Coding.completeTyping(s);
        Coding.repair(s, j, 0);
        Coding.preview(s, j, 2);
        Coding.deliver(s, j);
      }
      Life.buy(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.eat(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.advance(s, 360);
      Life.buy(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.eat(s, data.item("soup"));
      Life.sleep(s);
      assertTrue(s.hunger > 20);
      assertEquals(0, s.rentDebt);
    }
    assertEquals(10000 + 14 * (650 + 950 - 440) - 6000, s.money);
    assertEquals("08:00", s.clock());
  }

  @Test
  void passingNpcCannotStealDoorInteraction() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.map = "stair";
    s.x = 172.4f;
    s.y = 89.07f;
    var w = new World(content, s);
    assertNotNull(w.nearestNpc());
    assertEquals("door", w.nearestProp().action);
    assertNull(w.interactionNpc());
  }
}

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import life.ctrl.save.SaveStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class SaveTest {
  @TempDir Path dir;
  final Content content = Content.load();

  @Test
  void roundTripRetainsMapFlagsAppearanceAndClock() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    s.name = "Ёжик";
    s.outfit = 2;
    s.elapsed = 81.25;
    s.map = "street";
    s.x = 675;
    s.y = 310;
    s.hasLetter = true;
    s.inspectedPc = true;
    s.metMira = true;
    s.visited.add("street");
    s.direction = 2;
    saves.save(s);
    var copy = saves.load().state();
    assertEquals(s.name, copy.name);
    assertEquals(s.map, copy.map);
    assertEquals(s.x, copy.x);
    assertEquals(s.y, copy.y);
    assertEquals(s.elapsed, copy.elapsed);
    assertEquals(s.direction, copy.direction);
    assertEquals(s.outfit, copy.outfit);
    assertTrue(copy.hasLetter);
    assertTrue(copy.inspectedPc);
    assertEquals(s.visited, copy.visited);
  }

  @Test
  void backupRecoversCorruptPrimary() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    s.name = "Первый";
    saves.save(s);
    s.name = "Второй";
    saves.save(s);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "broken");
    var loaded = saves.load();
    assertTrue(loaded.fromBackup());
    assertEquals("Первый", loaded.state().name);
  }

  @Test
  void corruptPrimaryDoesNotOverwriteGoodBackup() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    saves.save(s);
    s.name = "Мира";
    saves.save(s);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "broken");
    s.name = "Лев";
    saves.save(s);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "broken again");
    assertEquals("Саша", saves.load().state().name);
  }

  @Test
  void corruptOrFutureSaveDoesNotCrashCaller() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("save.json"), "{\"version\":999,\"state\":{}}");
    assertThrows(java.io.IOException.class, saves::load);
  }

  @Test
  void savingInEveryMapWorks() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    for (var m : content.maps) {
      var s = new GameState();
      s.map = m.id;
      s.x = m.spawnX;
      s.y = m.spawnY;
      saves.save(s);
      assertEquals(m.id, saves.load().state().map);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void invalidPositionDoesNotReplaceSave() throws Exception {
    var saves = new SaveStore(dir, content);
    var s = new GameState();
    saves.save(s);
    s.x = -100;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> saves.save(s));
    assertEquals(240, saves.load().state().x);
  }

  @Test
  void settingsRoundTripAndBadFileFallback() throws Exception {
    var s = new Settings();
    s.music = .25f;
    s.reducedEffects = true;
    s.scale = 2;
    s.bind(0, com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys.T);
    s.save(dir);
    var copy = Settings.load(dir);
    assertEquals(s.up, copy.up);
    assertEquals(s.music, copy.music);
    assertTrue(copy.reducedEffects);
    Files.writeString(dir.resolve("settings.json"), "bad");
    assertEquals(3, Settings.load(dir).scale);
  }

  @Test
  void reservedAndDuplicateBindingsRejected() {
    var s = new Settings();
    assertFalse(s.bind(0, com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys.ESCAPE));
    assertFalse(s.bind(0, s.down));
    assertTrue(s.bind(0, com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys.T));
  }
}

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/VisualAssetTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.util.*;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.render.SceneLayout;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class VisualAssetTest {
  BufferedImage image(String path) throws Exception {
    try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + path + ".png")) {
      assertNotNull(in, path);
      return ImageIO.read(in);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void highDensityGeometryMatchesEveryMapAndProp() throws Exception {
    for (var map : Content.load().maps) {
      var hd = image("art/hd/" + map.id);
      assertEquals(map.width * 2, hd.getWidth());
      assertEquals(map.height * 2, hd.getHeight());
      var b = SceneLayout.bounds(map.id);
      assertTrue(
          b.x() >= 0
              && b.y() >= 0
              && b.x() + b.width() <= map.width
              && b.y() + b.height() <= map.height);
      for (var prop : map.objects)
        if (prop.art != null) {
          var old = image("art/" + prop.art);
          var p = image("art/hd/" + prop.art);
          assertEquals(old.getWidth() * 2, p.getWidth(), prop.art);
          assertEquals(old.getHeight() * 2, p.getHeight(), prop.art);
        }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void animationFramesCannotBleedIntoAdjacentCells() throws Exception {
    for (String id :
        List.of("hero", "hero_1", "hero_2", "mira", "asya", "nika", "tamara", "arkady", "lev")) {
      var p = image("art/hd/" + id);
      assertEquals(128, p.getWidth());
      assertEquals(192, p.getHeight());
      for (int row = 0; row < 4; row++)
        for (int col = 0; col < 4; col++) {
          for (int x = 0; x < 32; x++) {
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32 + x, row * 48) >>> 24, id);
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32 + x, row * 48 + 47) >>> 24, id);
          }
          for (int y = 0; y < 48; y++) {
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32, row * 48 + y) >>> 24, id);
            assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(col * 32 + 31, row * 48 + y) >>> 24, id);
          }
        }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void LightAndShadowTexturesFadeToTransparentAtAllEdges() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("light_amber", "light_monitor", "light_window", "shadow")) {
      var p = image("art/hd/" + id);
      for (int x = 0; x < p.getWidth(); x++) {
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(x, 0) >>> 24, id);
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(x, p.getHeight() - 1) >>> 24, id);
      }
      for (int y = 0; y < p.getHeight(); y++) {
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(0, y) >>> 24, id);
        assertEquals(0, p.getRGB(p.getWidth() - 1, y) >>> 24, id);
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void cutawayVoidIsOpaqueAndUnpainted() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("room", "stair", "cafe", "diner", "workshop", "park")) {
      var p = image("art/hd/" + id);
      var b = SceneLayout.bounds(id);
      for (int y = 0; y < p.getHeight(); y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < p.getWidth(); x++)
          if (x < b.x() * 2
              || y < b.y() * 2
              || x >= (b.x() + b.width()) * 2
              || y >= (b.y() + b.height()) * 2)
            assertEquals(0xff0e1a24, p.getRGB(x, y), id + " scene escaped its boundary");
    }
  }

  @Test
  void highDensityFontAtlasesFitBaselineGpuAndGlyphsStayInsideAtlas() throws Exception {
    for (String id : List.of("ui", "small", "title")) {
      var p = image("fonts/hd/" + id);
      assertTrue(p.getWidth() <= 2048 && p.getHeight() <= 2048, id);
      try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/fonts/hd/" + id + ".fnt")) {
        String text = new String(in.readAllBytes(), java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        var match =
            java.util.regex.Pattern.compile(
                    "char id=([0-9]+) x=([0-9]+) y=([0-9]+) width=([0-9]+) height=([0-9]+)")
                .matcher(text);
        int count = 0;
        while (match.find()) {
          int x = Integer.parseInt(match.group(2)), y = Integer.parseInt(match.group(3));
          assertTrue(
              x + Integer.parseInt(match.group(4)) <= p.getWidth(),
              id + " glyph " + match.group(1));
          assertTrue(
              y + Integer.parseInt(match.group(5)) <= p.getHeight(),
              id + " glyph " + match.group(1));
          count++;
        }
        assertTrue(count >= 200, id + " Cyrillic glyphs missing");
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void oldSaveInsideNewFurnitureMovesToEntranceWithoutLosingProgress() throws Exception {
    java.nio.file.Path folder = java.nio.file.Files.createTempDirectory("ctrl-visual-migration");
    try {
      java.nio.file.Files.writeString(
          folder.resolve("save.json"),
          "{\"version\":3,\"state\":{\"map\":\"diner\",\"x\":350,\"y\":145,\"money\":4321,\"codingXp\":8,\"completedOrders\":6,\"shipped\":[\"total\"],\"upgrades\":[\"ssd\"]}}");
      var store = new life.ctrl.save.SaveStore(folder, Content.load());
      var state = store.load().state();
      assertEquals(240, state.x);
      assertEquals(248, state.y);
      assertEquals(4321, state.money);
      assertEquals(8, state.codingXp);
      assertEquals(6, state.completedOrders);
      assertTrue(state.shipped.contains("total"));
      assertTrue(state.upgrades.contains("ssd"));
      store.save(state);
      assertEquals(4321, store.load().state().money);
    } finally {
      try (var files = java.nio.file.Files.list(folder)) {
        for (var file : files.toList()) java.nio.file.Files.delete(file);
      }
      java.nio.file.Files.delete(folder);
    }
  }
}

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/WorldTest.java

package life.ctrl;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.util.*;
import life.ctrl.content.Content;
import life.ctrl.model.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class WorldTest {
  final Content content = Content.load();

  World fresh() {
    return new World(content, new GameState());
  }

  @Test
  void contentReferencesAndEffectsAreValid() {
    content.validate();
    World world = fresh();
    for (var d : content.dialogues)
      for (var c : d.choices) assertDoesNotThrow(() -> world.effect(c.effect));
    for (var m : content.maps)
      for (var p : m.objects)
        if (!Set.of("door", "decoration", "wardrobe", "rest", "shop", "date", "courier", "wait")
            .contains(p.action)) assertDoesNotThrow(() -> content.dialogue(world.propDialogue(p)));
  }

  @Test
  void diagonalMovementIsNotFaster() {
    var straight = fresh();
    var diagonal = fresh();
    for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
      straight.tick(1, 0);
      diagonal.tick(1, 1);
    }
    assertEquals(
        Math.hypot(straight.state.x - 240, straight.state.y - 190),
        Math.hypot(diagonal.state.x - 240, diagonal.state.y - 190),
        .002);
  }

  @Test
  void walkingIntoWallStopsWithoutTunnelling() {
    var w = fresh();
    for (int i = 0; i < 10000; i++) w.tick(1, 0);
    assertFalse(w.blocked(w.state.x, w.state.y));
    assertTrue(w.state.x < 416);
    assertTrue(w.state.x > 300);
  }

  @Test
  void equalTickCountsGiveEqualTimeAndDistance() {
    var a = fresh();
    var b = fresh();
    for (int frame = 0; frame < 30; frame++) {
      a.tick(-1, 0);
      a.tick(-1, 0);
    }
    for (int frame = 0; frame < 60; frame++) b.tick(-1, 0);
    assertEquals(a.state.x, b.state.x);
    assertEquals(a.state.elapsed, b.state.elapsed);
  }

  @Test
  void doorsHaveValidSpawnPositionsAndConnectEveryMap() {
    Set<String> visited = new HashSet<>();
    ArrayDeque<String> todo = new ArrayDeque<>();
    todo.add("room");
    while (!todo.isEmpty()) {
      String id = todo.remove();
      if (!visited.add(id)) continue;
      for (var p : content.map(id).objects)
        if (p.action.equals("door")) {
          GameState s = new GameState();
          s.map = id;
          s.x = content.map(id).spawnX;
          s.y = content.map(id).spawnY;
          World w = new World(content, s);
          w.enter(p);
          assertFalse(w.blocked(s.x, s.y), id + " -> " + p.target);
          todo.add(p.target);
          assertTrue(
              Arrays.stream(content.map(p.target).objects).anyMatch(q -> id.equals(q.target)),
              "Return door absent: " + id);
        }
    }
    assertEquals(content.maps.length, visited.size());
  }

  @Test
  void everyInteractionCanBeReachedFromMapEntry() {
    for (var map : content.maps) {
      GameState s = new GameState();
      s.map = map.id;
      s.x = map.spawnX;
      s.y = map.spawnY;
      World w = new World(content, s);
      int step = 4, cols = map.width / step, rows = map.height / step;
      boolean[] reachable = new boolean[cols * rows];
      ArrayDeque<Integer> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();
      int start = Math.round(s.y / step) * cols + Math.round(s.x / step);
      reachable[start] = true;
      queue.add(start);
      while (!queue.isEmpty()) {
        int at = queue.remove(), x = at % cols, y = at / cols;
        for (int[] d : new int[][] {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}}) {
          int nx = x + d[0], ny = y + d[1];
          if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= cols || ny >= rows) continue;
          int next = ny * cols + nx;
          if (!reachable[next] && !w.blocked(nx * step, ny * step)) {
            reachable[next] = true;
            queue.add(next);
          }
        }
      }
      for (var p : map.objects) {
        if (p.action.equals("decoration")) continue;
        boolean found = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < reachable.length; i++)
          if (reachable[i] && Math.hypot((i % cols) * step - p.x, (i / cols) * step - p.y) < 28) {
            found = true;
            break;
          }
        assertTrue(found, map.id + " unreachable: " + p.id);
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void npcRoutesStayWalkableAndDeterministic() {
    for (var map : content.maps) {
      GameState s = new GameState();
      s.map = map.id;
      s.x = map.spawnX;
      s.y = map.spawnY;
      World w = new World(content, s);
      var a = new World.Actor();
      var b = new World.Actor();
      for (int t = 0; t < 500; t++) {
        s.elapsed = t * .25;
        for (var npc : map.npcs) {
          w.position(npc, a);
          w.position(npc, b);
          assertEquals(a.x, b.x);
          assertEquals(a.y, b.y);
          assertFalse(w.blocked(a.x, a.y), npc.id + " intersects wall at " + a.x + "," + a.y);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  void letterCanBeFoundBeforeQuestAndCannotBeDeliveredTwice() {
    var w = fresh();
    w.effect("deliverLetter");
    assertFalse(w.state.deliveredLetter);
    w.effect("takeLetter");
    assertTrue(w.state.hasLetter);
    w.effect("deliverLetter");
    w.effect("deliverLetter");
    w.effect("takeLetter");
    assertTrue(w.state.deliveredLetter);
    assertFalse(w.state.hasLetter);
    assertEquals("miraAgain", w.npcDialogue(content.map("stair").npcs[0]));
  }

  @Test
  void guideUsesReachableDoorsFromEveryMap() {
    var catalog = life.ctrl.content.LifeContent.load();
    for (var map : content.maps) {
      var state = new GameState();
      state.map = map.id;
      state.x = map.spawnX;
      state.y = map.spawnY;
      var w = new World(content, state);
      var g = Navigation.localGoal(w, Navigation.goal(w, catalog));
      assertEquals(map.id, g.map());
      assertTrue(
          Math.hypot(state.x - g.x(), state.y - g.y()) < 29
              || !Navigation.route(w, g.x(), g.y()).isEmpty(),
          map.id);
    }
  }

  @Test
  void nearbyNpcHasContextualDialogue() {
    var w = fresh();
    var npc = content.map("stair").npcs[0];
    assertEquals("mira", w.npcDialogue(npc));
    w.effect("takeLetter");
    assertEquals("miraFound", w.npcDialogue(npc));
  }

  @Test
  void malformedStatesAreRejected() {
    var s = new GameState();
    s.x = Float.NaN;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
    s.x = 10;
    s.y = 10;
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
    s.map = "absent";
    assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new World(content, s));
  }
}
END FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/WorldTest.java

FILE: core/src/test/java/life/ctrl/ui/PinuxWindowManagerTest.java

package life.ctrl.ui;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class PinuxWindowManagerTest {
  @Test
  void windowsRetainIndependentFocusAndMinimization() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    assertNull(wm.front());
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL));
    wm.minimize(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE));
    assertTrue(wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL).visible);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertFalse(wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL).minimized);
    wm.close(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE));
    assertFalse(wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL).visible);
  }

  @Test
  void draggingRestoresMaximizedWindowAndKeepsCaptionVisible() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    wm.toggleMaximize(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    assertTrue(wm.isMaximizedCode());
    var w = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.x + 56, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDragged(265, 115));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerUp(265, 115));
    assertFalse(w.maximized);
    assertTrue(w.y >= PinuxWindowManager.TOP + 1);
    assertTrue(w.x >= 0);
    assertTrue(w.x + w.width <= 480);
    assertTrue(w.y + 25 <= PinuxWindowManager.BOTTOM);
  }

  @Test
  void captionButtonsMinimizeMaximizeAndCloseWithoutClickThrough() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    var w = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 22, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(w.maximized);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 36, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(w.minimized);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 7, w.y + 8));
    assertFalse(w.visible);
  }

  @Test
  void cornerResizeClampsToDesktopAndEdgeDraggingSnaps() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    var w = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.TERMINAL);
    int initialWidth = w.width;
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.right() - 3, w.bottom() - 3));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDragged(w.right() + 50, w.bottom() + 15));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerUp(w.right(), w.bottom()));
    assertTrue(w.width >= initialWidth);
    assertTrue(w.right() <= 480);
    assertTrue(w.bottom() <= PinuxWindowManager.BOTTOM);

    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(w.x + 40, w.y + 8));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDragged(1, 90));
    assertTrue(wm.pointerUp(1, 90));
    assertEquals(2, w.x);
    assertEquals(236, w.width);
  }

  @Test
  void clickingBackgroundWindowFocusesBeforeItsControls() {
    var wm = new PinuxWindowManager();
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    wm.open(PinuxWindowManager.App.FILES);
    var code = wm.get(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE);
    // Far-right lower region belongs to CODE but is not covered by FILES.
    assertTrue(wm.pointerDown(code.right() - 8, code.bottom() - 12));
    assertTrue(wm.isFront(PinuxWindowManager.App.CODE));
  }
}

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;
import life.ctrl.model.*;

/**
 * Actual input and rendering for the complete first day. Later intimacy UI uses an explicit
 * fixture.
 */
public final class ChapterSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  private record Step(String label, BooleanSupplier run) {}

  private final ArrayDeque<Step> steps = new ArrayDeque<>();
  private final ArrayList<float[]> route = new ArrayList<>();
  private int frames;
  private float timer, total;
  private boolean failed;
  private String current = "startup";
  private float beforeX, beforeY;
  private double pauseTime;

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path dir = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(dir);
    for (String n : List.of("save.json", "save.backup.json", "settings.json"))
      Files.deleteIfExists(dir.resolve(n));
    Files.createDirectories(Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots")));
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.setTitle("CTRL+LIFE 0.2 — test");
    config.disableAudio(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio"));
    config.setForegroundFPS(Integer.getInteger("ctrl.fps", 60));
    config.useVsync(false);
    ChapterSmoke game = new ChapterSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, config);
    if (game.failed) throw new AssertionError(game.current);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      if (Files.exists(dir.resolve("save.json"))) throw new AssertionError("Menu created a save");
      System.out.println("MENU_ONLY_PASS");
    }
  }

  private void add(String name, BooleanSupplier f) {
    steps.add(new Step(name, f));
  }

  private void once(String name, Runnable f) {
    add(
        name,
        () -> {
          if (timer < .08f) return false;
          f.run();
          return true;
        });
  }

  private void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  private void click(int x, int y) {
    Gdx.input
        .getInputProcessor()
        .touchDown(
            canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale,
            canvas().offsetY + y * canvas().scale,
            0,
            Input.Buttons.LEFT);
  }

  private void stop() {
    for (int k : new int[] {Keys.W, Keys.A, Keys.S, Keys.D}) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(k);
  }

  private void check(boolean ok, String message) {
    if (!ok) throw new AssertionError(message);
  }

  private void shot(String name) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/" + name + ".png");
  }

  private void waitFor(Mode mode) {
    add("wait " + mode, () -> mode() == mode);
  }

  private void pressE() {
    once(
        "interact",
        () -> {
          stop();
          tap(Keys.E);
        });
  }

  private void closeScene() {
    waitFor(Mode.SCENE);
    once("continue scene", () -> click(240, 237));
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
  }

  private void walk(float x, float y) {
    once(
        "plan " + x + "," + y,
        () -> {
          route.clear();
          route.addAll(Navigation.route(world(), x, y));
          check(
              !route.isEmpty() || Math.hypot(world().state.x - x, world().state.y - y) < 29,
              "No route");
        });
    add(
        "walk " + x + "," + y,
        () -> {
          stop();
          if (route.isEmpty()) return true;
          var p = route.get(0);
          float dx = p[0] - world().state.x, dy = p[1] - world().state.y;
          if (Math.abs(dx) < 1.6 && Math.abs(dy) < 1.6) {
            route.remove(0);
            return route.isEmpty();
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) >= 1.3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) >= 1.3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
  }

  private void approachMira() {
    add(
        "approach Mira",
        () -> {
          stop();
          var npc =
              world().npcs().stream().filter(n -> n.id.equals("mira")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
          var a = new World.Actor();
          world().position(npc, a);
          float dx = a.x - world().state.x, dy = a.y - world().state.y;
          if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 22) {
            tap(Keys.E);
            return true;
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) > 3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) > 3) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
    waitFor(Mode.SOCIAL);
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    add("boot", () -> frames > 4);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      once("settings", () -> click(300, 193));
      waitFor(Mode.SETTINGS);
      once("close settings without game", () -> Gdx.app.exit());
      return;
    }
    once(
        "menu",
        () -> {
          shot("01-menu");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.CREATE);
    once(
        "new game",
        () -> {
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.INTRO);
    once(
        "arrival",
        () -> {
          shot("02-arrival");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    once("budget introduction", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    once(
        "controls introduction",
        () -> {
          shot("03-onboarding");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
    once("poor room", () -> shot("04-room"));
    walk(187, 156);
    pressE();
    waitFor(Mode.IDE);
    once(
        "job board",
        () -> {
          shot("05-jobs");
          click(240, 122);
        });
    once("leave before algorithm", () -> tap(Keys.ESCAPE));
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
    pressE();
    waitFor(Mode.IDE);
    once(
        "reopen unstarted order",
        () -> {
          check(lifeScreens().codePage == 0, "Unstarted order stuck in editor");

        () -> {
          shot("06-algorithm");

        });

          beforeX = world().state.x;
          beforeY = world().state.y;
          for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) tap(i % 2 == 0 ? Keys.W : Keys.E);
        });
    once(
        "typing isolated",
        () -> {
          check(beforeX == world().state.x && beforeY == world().state.y, "IDE moved player");
          click(180, 236);
        });
    once(

        "fade to black",

    once(
        "morning memory",
        () ->
            check(
                world().state.relation("mira").sharedNights == 1,
                "Night consent outcome not saved"));
    once("contacts", () -> tap(Keys.R));
    waitFor(Mode.CONTACTS);
    once(
        "contacts portraits",
        () -> {
          shot("19-contacts");
          tap(Keys.ESCAPE);
        });
    waitFor(Mode.WORLD);
    once("small window", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(960, 540));
    add("resize", () -> timer > .3);
    once("960 shot", () -> shot("20-window-960"));
    once("large window", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(1920, 1080));
    add("resize large", () -> timer > .3);
    once("1080 shot", () -> shot("21-window-1080"));
    add(
        "soak",
        () -> {
          check(!world().blocked(world().state.x, world().state.y), "Invalid world position");
          return total >= Float.parseFloat(System.getProperty("ctrl.soakSeconds", "0"));
        });
    once(
        "complete",
        () -> {
          System.out.println(
              "CHAPTER_SMOKE_PASS frames="
                  + frames
                  + " seconds="
                  + total
                  + " first day + labelled consent fixture");
          Gdx.app.exit();
        });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    frames++;
    float dt = Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    timer += dt;
    total += dt;
    if (steps.isEmpty()) return;
    var step = steps.peek();
    current = step.label;
    try {
      if (step.run.getAsBoolean()) {
        System.out.println("GUI: " + current);
        steps.remove();
        timer = 0;
      } else if (timer > 60 && !current.equals("soak"))
        throw new AssertionError("Timeout " + current);
    } catch (Throwable e) {
      failed = true;
      shot("FAILED");
      e.printStackTrace();
      steps.clear();
      Gdx.app.exit();
    }
  }
}

package life.ctrl.desktop;

import com.badlogic.gdx.*;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input.Keys;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;
import life.ctrl.CtrlLife;
import life.ctrl.model.Settings;
import life.ctrl.model.World;

/** Runs actual LWJGL3 rendering and keyboard input. Requires an OpenGL-capable display. */
public final class GuiSmoke extends CtrlLife {
  private final ArrayDeque<Step> script = new ArrayDeque<>();
  private final ArrayList<float[]> path = new ArrayList<>();
  private long started;
  private float stepTime, total, beforeMove;
  private double beforePause;
  private int frames;
  private boolean failed, soakRight = true;
  private String current = "startup";

  private record Step(String name, BooleanSupplier run) {}

  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
    Path save = Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.dataDir"));
    Files.createDirectories(save);
    for (String f : new String[] {"save.json", "save.backup.json", "settings.json"})
      Files.deleteIfExists(save.resolve(f));
    Files.createDirectories(Path.of(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots")));
    var config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
    config.setTitle("CTRL+LIFE — GUI smoke");
    config.setWindowedMode(1440, 810);
    config.disableAudio(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio"));
    config.setForegroundFPS(Integer.getInteger("ctrl.fps", 60));
    config.useVsync(false);
    GuiSmoke game = new GuiSmoke();
    new Lwjgl3Application(game, config);
    if (game.failed) throw new IllegalStateException("GUI smoke failed: " + game.current);
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      if (Files.exists(save.resolve("save.json")))
        throw new AssertionError("Menu-only session created a save");
      System.out.println(
          "GUI_MENU_ONLY_PASS: closing settings without starting does not create a save");
    }
  }

  private void add(String name, BooleanSupplier f) {
    script.add(new Step(name, f));
  }

  private void tap(int key) {
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(key);
    Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(key);
  }

  private void click(int x, int y) {
    Gdx.input
        .getInputProcessor()
        .touchDown(
            canvas().offsetX + x * canvas().scale,
            canvas().offsetY + y * canvas().scale,
            0,
            Input.Buttons.LEFT);
  }

  private void stop() {
    for (int k : new int[] {Keys.W, Keys.A, Keys.S, Keys.D}) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(k);
  }

  private void shot(String name) {
    canvas().screenshot(System.getProperty("ctrl.shots") + "/" + name + ".png");
  }

  private void check(boolean value, String error) {
    if (!value) throw new AssertionError(error);
  }

  private void once(String name, Runnable action) {
    add(
        name,
        () -> {
          action.run();
          return true;
        });
  }

  private void waitMode(Mode mode) {
    add("wait " + mode, () -> mode() == mode);
  }

  private void walk(float x, float y) {
    once("path to " + x + "," + y, () -> plan(x, y));
    add(
        "walk to " + x + "," + y,
        () -> {
          stop();
          if (path.isEmpty()) return true;
          var target = path.get(0);
          float dx = target[0] - world().state.x, dy = target[1] - world().state.y;
          if (Math.abs(dx) < 1.6 && Math.abs(dy) < 1.6) {
            path.remove(0);
            return path.isEmpty();
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) > 1) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) > 1) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
  }

  private void choose(int n) {
    once("choose " + n, () -> tap(Keys.NUM_1 + n - 1));
  }

  private void interact() {
    once(
        "interact",
        () -> {
          stop();
          tap(Keys.E);
        });
  }

  private void plan(float tx, float ty) {
    path.clear();
    World w = world();
    int grid = 4, cols = w.map().width / grid, rows = w.map().height / grid;
    int start = Math.round(w.state.y / grid) * cols + Math.round(w.state.x / grid);
    int[] prev = new int[cols * rows];
    Arrays.fill(prev, -1);
    prev[start] = start;
    ArrayDeque<Integer> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
    q.add(start);
    int goal = -1;
    while (!q.isEmpty()) {
      int at = q.remove(), x = at % cols, y = at / cols;
      if (Math.hypot(x * grid - tx, y * grid - ty) < 3) {
        goal = at;
        break;
      }
      for (int[] d : new int[][] {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}}) {
        int nx = x + d[0], ny = y + d[1];
        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= cols || ny >= rows) continue;
        int next = ny * cols + nx;
        if (prev[next] < 0
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid - 2, ny * grid - 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid + 2, ny * grid - 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid - 2, ny * grid + 2)
            && !w.blocked(nx * grid + 2, ny * grid + 2)) {
          prev[next] = at;
          q.add(next);
        }
      }
    }
    check(goal >= 0, "No path to " + tx + "," + ty);
    ArrayList<float[]> reverse = new ArrayList<>();
    for (int at = goal; at != start; at = prev[at])
      reverse.add(new float[] {at % cols * grid, at / cols * grid});
    Collections.reverse(reverse);
    path.addAll(reverse);
  }

  @Override
  public void create() {
    super.create();
    started = System.nanoTime();
    check(Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.noAudio") || audioReady(), "Audio assets did not load");
    if (Boolean.getBoolean("ctrl.menuOnly")) {
      add("wait boot", () -> frames > 5);
      once("open settings", () -> click(300, 193));
      waitMode(Mode.SETTINGS);
      once("close without playing", () -> Gdx.app.exit());
      return;
    }
    add("wait boot", () -> frames > 5);
    once(
        "menu screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("01-menu");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.CREATE);
    once(
        "create screenshot",
        () -> {
          shot("02-create");
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("room screenshot", () -> shot("03-room"));
    walk(392, 116);
    interact();
    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    choose(1);
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("plant watered", () -> check(world().state.wateredPlant, "Watering did not persist"));
    walk(155, 240);
    interact();
    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    choose(1);
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("boxes unpacked", () -> check(world().state.unpacked, "Unpacking did not persist"));
    walk(110, 196);

    once(
        "outfit changed", () -> check(world().state.outfit == 1, "Wardrobe did not change outfit"));
    walk(128, 140);

    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    once("computer screenshot", () -> shot("04-computer-dialogue"));
    choose(1);
    choose(1);
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("computer flag", () -> check(world().state.inspectedPc, "PC was not inspected"));
    walk(243, 252);
    interact();
    once(
        "entered stair",
        () -> check(world().state.map.equals("stair"), "Door did not enter stair"));
    // Approach the moving NPC through actual movement; dialogue freezes the world clock.
    add(
        "approach Mira",
        () -> {
          stop();
          var a = new World.Actor();
          world().position(world().map().npcs[0], a);
          float dx = a.x - world().state.x, dy = a.y - world().state.y;
          if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 23) {
            tap(Keys.E);
            return true;
          }
          if (Math.abs(dx) > 4) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dx > 0 ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          if (Math.abs(dy) > 4) Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(dy > 0 ? Keys.S : Keys.W);
          return false;
        });
    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);
    once("Mira screenshot", () -> shot("05-mira"));
    choose(1);
    choose(1);
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("Mira request", () -> check(world().state.askedLetter, "Mira quest not accepted"));
    walk(244, 254);
    interact();
    once(
        "street screenshot",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.map.equals("street"), "Door did not enter street");

    walk(480, 287);
    once("street middle screenshot", () -> shot("07-street-workshop"));
    walk(675, 310);
    interact();
    waitMode(Mode.DIALOGUE);

    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);

        "letter collected",

          check(world().state.hasLetter, "Letter not collected");

          tap(Keys.I);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.BAG);
    once(
        "inventory screenshot",
        () -> {

          tap(Keys.ESCAPE);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once("map", () -> tap(Keys.M));
    waitMode(Mode.MAP);

        "map screenshot",

          shot("10-map");
          tap(Keys.ESCAPE);

    walk(174, 235);

          var a = new World.Actor();
          world().position(world().map().npcs[0], a);

          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(Keys.D);
          focusChanged(false);
          check(mode() == Mode.PAUSE, "Focus did not pause");
          check(world().state.x == x, "Unexpected movement");
          focusChanged(true);

        "paused time check",

          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.CONTROLS);

        "controls screenshot",

          shot("14-controls");
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });

    once(
        "back menu",
        () -> {
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.DOWN);
          tap(Keys.ENTER);
        });
    waitMode(Mode.MENU);
    once("continue", () -> tap(Keys.ENTER));
    waitMode(Mode.WORLD);
    once(
        "restored",
        () -> {
          check(world().state.deliveredLetter, "Continue lost quest state");
          check(
              world().state.wateredPlant && world().state.unpacked && world().state.outfit == 1,
              "Continue lost object changes");
          check(world().state.map.equals("room"), "Continue lost map");
        });
    once(
        "move with remapped T",
        () -> {
          beforeMove = world().state.y;
          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(Keys.T);
        });
    add("hold remapped key", () -> stepTime > .25);
    once(
        "remapped movement works",
        () -> {
          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyUp(Keys.T);
          check(world().state.y < beforeMove - 8, "Remapped key did not move player");
        });
    once("960 resize", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(960, 540));
    add("resize settle", () -> stepTime > .3);
    once("small screenshot", () -> shot("15-window-960"));
    once("1080 resize", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(1920, 1080));
    add("large settle", () -> stepTime > .3);
    once("1080 screenshot", () -> shot("16-window-1080"));
    once("restore size", () -> Gdx.graphics.setWindowedMode(1440, 810));
    add(
        "soak",
        () -> {
          check(!world().blocked(world().state.x, world().state.y), "Invalid position during soak");
          stop();
          if (total >= Float.parseFloat(System.getProperty("ctrl.soakSeconds", "0"))) return true;
          if (world().state.x > 275) soakRight = false;
          if (world().state.x < 180) soakRight = true;
          Gdx.input.getInputProcessor().keyDown(soakRight ? Keys.D : Keys.A);
          return false;
        });
    once(
        "finished",
        () -> {
          System.out.println(
              "GUI_SMOKE_PASS: movement, 3 maps, dialogues, quest, save/continue, focus, menus,"
                  + " resize. Frames="
                  + frames
                  + " seconds="
                  + total);
          Gdx.app.exit();
        });
  }

  @Override
  public void render() {
    super.render();
    frames++;
    float dt = Gdx.graphics.getDeltaTime();
    stepTime += dt;
    total = (System.nanoTime() - started) / 1e9f;
    if (script.isEmpty()) return;
    Step step = script.peek();
    current = step.name;
    try {
      if (step.run.getAsBoolean()) {
        System.out.println("GUI: " + current);
        script.remove();
        stepTime = 0;
      } else if (stepTime > 45 && !current.equals("soak"))
        throw new AssertionError("Timeout " + current);
    } catch (Throwable e) {
      failed = true;
      shot("FAILED");
      e.printStackTrace();
      Gdx.app.exit();
      script.clear();
    }
  }
}
